## Portal Path Endpoint: SEC
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
The student portal section named 'SEC' appears to handle various academic and administrative tasks for students. Here is a detailed breakdown of the features, data visible, and how this portion of the portal works:

### Features:
1. **Schedule**: Displays upcoming events and lessons.
2. **Circulars**: Shows notifications or important announcements.
3. **My Reward Points**: Tracks and manages student rewards or points.
4. **Workflow Requests**: Manages and tracks student requests or applications.
5. **Workflow Approvals**: Tracks and manages student approvals or decisions.
6. **Bookings**: Manages student bookings or appointments.
7. **Services**: Provides access to various student services.
8. **Mentees**: Manages student-mentee relationships.
9. **Mentorship**: Manages student-mentor relationships.
10. **Logged in as**: Displays the user's login information.

### Data Visible:
1. **Event - VIVA**:
   - Type: Examinations
   - Purpose: C Module 11
   - Time: 09:00 AM - 09:59 AM
   - Venue: 1611
   - Cancellation Ends: August 14, 2026, 8 AM
   - Status: Cancellation Closed

2. **19CS406 - Computer Networks**:
   - Slot: 26OD1001
   - Lesson: CIA 1
   - Time: 10:00 AM - 11:59 AM
   - Venue: Not Available

3. **Event - Module/LeetCode/SA**:
   - Type: Examinations
   - Purpose: Skill assessment
   - Time: 10:00 AM - 11:29 AM
   - Venue: 4731

### How This Portion of the Portal Works:
1. **Event Management**: The portal allows students to view and manage their upcoming events, such as exams, lessons, and skill assessments. Each event includes details like type, purpose, time, venue, and cancellation status.

2. **Circulars and Notifications**: Students can receive important announcements or notifications through the 'Circulars' section. This helps keep students informed about upcoming events, changes in schedules, or other important information.

3. **Reward Points**: The 'My Reward Points' section tracks and manages student rewards or points. This could be used to incentivize students or track their academic progress.

4. **Workflow Requests and Approvals**: Students can submit requests or applications through the 'Workflow Requests' and 'Workflow Approvals' sections. These sections help manage and track the status of these requests, ensuring they are processed efficiently.

5. **Bookings and Services**: The 'Bookings' and 'Services' sections allow students to manage their appointments or access various student services. This could include things like counseling, tutoring, or other academic support.

6. **Mentees and Mentorship**: The 'Mentees' and 'Mentorship' sections manage student-mentee and student-mentor relationships. This could be used to provide guidance, support, or mentorship to students.

7. **Logged in as**: Displays the user's login information, including their ID, name, and ref numbers. This helps in identifying the user and ensuring they have the necessary permissions to access the portal.

Overall, the 'SEC' section of the student portal is designed to provide a comprehensive view of a student's academic and administrative activities, ensuring they have all the necessary information and tools to manage their studies effectively.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Activity Log
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/people/activity-log/
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Feature Handling:
The 'Activity Log' section in the student portal handles the tracking and recording of activities and events that have occurred within the system. It provides a log of all activities that have taken place, allowing users to review their past actions and interactions with the system.

### Data Visible:
1. **Date/Time**: The date and time of the activity.
2. **Activity**: A brief description of the activity that took place.
3. **Details**: Additional information or context about the activity.
4. **IP Address**: The IP address of the device or server where the activity was recorded.

### How This Portion of the Portal Works:
1. **Logging Activities**: When a user performs an action within the system, such as submitting a form, creating a new record, or interacting with a specific feature, the system logs this activity.
2. **Displaying Logs**: The 'Activity Log' section displays these logged activities in chronological order, allowing users to review their past actions.
3. **Filtering and Sorting**: Users can filter the logs by date, activity type, or IP address to find specific entries.
4. **Notification**: If no activity is recorded in the last seven days, a message is displayed indicating that no activity has been recorded during that period.

### Detailed Explanation:
- **Date/Time**: This column shows when the activity occurred, helping users understand the sequence of events.
- **Activity**: This column provides a brief description of what the user did, making it easy to identify the nature of the activity.
- **Details**: This column offers additional information or context, such as the specific task or document being worked on.
- **IP Address**: This column shows the device or server where the activity was recorded, which can be useful for troubleshooting or security purposes.

### Example of How This Works in Practice:
- **User Logs In**: When a user logs in, the system records this activity in the log.
- **User Submits a Form**: When a user submits a form, the system logs this action with the date and time, the type of activity (submitting a form), and the details of the form.
- **User Reviews Logs**: The user can review the activity log to see all their past actions, helping them understand their interactions with the system.

This section is crucial for maintaining transparency and accountability within the system, ensuring that users can trace their actions and understand the history of their interactions.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Active Sessions
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/people/active-sessions/
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Feature: Active Sessions
This section of the student portal is designed to manage and monitor the active sessions of users who are currently signed into the system. It provides a way to review and control the sessions of users who are using the portal.

### Data Visible:
1. **Browser / Device:**
   - The type of browser or device used to access the portal.
   - For example, "Chrome 151.0.7922.34" for the current session.

2. **Session Started:**
   - The date and time when the session was started.
   - For example, "Aug 13, 2026 19:45:14".

3. **Last Accessed:**
   - The date and time when the session was last accessed.
   - For example, "Aug 14, 2026 23:30:46".

4. **IP Address:**
   - The IP address of the device used to access the portal.
   - For example, "10.0.0.7".

5. **Action:**
   - Options to take actions on the session, such as "Use Logout to end this session" or "Invalidate".

### How This Portion of the Portal Works:
1. **Session Management:**
   - Users can see a list of all active sessions they are currently logged into.
   - They can also see the details of each session, including the browser/device used, the date and time of session start and end, and the IP address.

2. **Session Control:**
   - Users have the option to "Use Logout to end this session" to terminate the current session.
   - They also have the option to "Invalidate" a session, which can be useful for terminating a session that is no longer needed or for security reasons.

3. **User Interface:**
   - The interface is designed to be user-friendly, with clear labels and options to manage sessions.
   - Users can easily navigate through the sessions and take the necessary actions.

4. **Security:**
   - The section helps in maintaining security by allowing users to terminate sessions that are no longer needed or to invalidate sessions that might be compromised.

### Summary:
The "Active Sessions" section is a crucial feature of the student portal, providing users with the ability to manage and control their active sessions. It helps in maintaining system security and user control over their access to the portal.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Logout

### Data Visible:
1. **Session Ended Notification**: The notification indicates that the session has been securely closed.
2. **User Status**: The user is now fully signed out.
3. **Options to Sign In Again or Return to Home**: Two buttons are provided for the user to either sign in again or return to the home page.

### How This Portion of the Portal Works:
1. **Session Securely Closed**: When the user clicks the logout button, the system ensures that the session is securely closed. This typically involves removing session cookies, clearing session data, and terminating the session.
2. **User Notification**: A confirmation message is displayed to the user, informing them that their session has been securely closed and that they are now fully signed out.
3. **User Options**: The user is given two options:
   - **Sign in again**: Allows the user to re-enter their credentials and sign in again.
   - **Back to home**: Redirects the user to the home page of the portal, where they can start a new session.

This process ensures that the user's session is properly terminated and that they are no longer logged in, maintaining the security and privacy of the system.

   - This step is important for ensuring that students leave the venue after the event has ended.

6. **Status Tracking:**
   - The portal tracks the status of each step (IN OTP Entry, OUT OTP Entry) and the overall event status.
   - This helps in managing the event smoothly and ensuring that all students are accounted for.

### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Home Page Layout Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
The page appears to be a student portal for an academic event, specifically related to the "26-27 ODD EXAMCENTRE EXAMINATIONS." The page controls various transactional parameters such as booking, attendance, and feedback for the event. Key actions include:
- **Booking**: The student has booked a session for the event.
- **Attendance**: The student's attendance is marked as present.
- **Feedback**: The feedback has been posted.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is divided into several sections, each containing specific data elements:
- **Header**: Displays the event details, including the module, session date, and booking information.
- **Main Content Area**:
  - **Overview**: Displays the student's name, registration number, mode of booking, and session details.
  - **Summary**: Shows the attendance status and percentage.
  - **Feedback**: Indicates that the feedback has been posted.
- **Sidebar**: Contains navigation links for different sections of the portal.

#### 3. Score Layout and Variable Values
- **Event Details**:
  - **Module/LeetCode/SA**
  - **Session Date**: Aug 11, 2026
  - **Time**: 10 a.m. - 11:29 a.m.
  - **Location**: 4731
  - **Event Term**: 26-27 ODD EXAMCENTRE
- **Student Details**:
  - **Name**: Muhammad Asjad E
  - **Registration Number**: 212225240091
  - **Mode**: Booked
- **Attendance Status**:
  - **Status**: Present
  - **Attendance Percentage**: 100%
- **Feedback Status**:
  - **Status**: Feedback Posted

#### 4. Structural Functionality
- **Booking Information**: The booking information is displayed prominently, allowing students to view and manage their session bookings.
- **Attendance Tracking**: The attendance status is clearly indicated, ensuring transparency in the student's participation.
- **Feedback Submission**: The feedback section allows students to submit their feedback, which is then posted on the system.
- **Navigation Links**: The sidebar provides easy access to other sections of the portal, such as schedule, circulars, reward points, workflow requests, and workflow approvals.

### Summary
This page serves as a comprehensive interface for students to manage their participation in academic events. It ensures that students can easily view their booking details, attendance status, and feedback submissions. The structured layout and clear indicators help in maintaining transparency and accountability in the academic process.

- **Header**: Displays the event details, including the event ID, peer ID, event type, session date, time, location, booking status, and feedback status.
- **Student Information**: Displays the student's name, registration number, mode of attendance, and booking status.
- **Session Details**: Provides the session details, including the module/leet code, event term, location, and time.
- **Attendance Summary**: Shows the attendance percentage and status for the session.

- **Event ID**: 12947
- **Peer ID**: 1145

- **Location**: 4731
- **Booking Status**: Booked
- **Feedback Status**: Feedback Posted
- **Attendance Status**: Present
- **Attendance Percentage**: 100%

#### 4. Structural Functionality

- **Displaying Event Details**: Ensures that all relevant information about the event is visible to the student and the system administrator.
- **Student Information**: Provides the student's details, which can be used for follow-up or additional tracking.
- **Session Details**: Offers comprehensive information about the session, which can be useful for administrative purposes.
- **Attendance Summary**: Indicates the student's attendance status, which is crucial for evaluating the student's participation.
- **Feedback Summary**: Allows for the posting and viewing of feedback, which can be used for improvement and communication.
- **Booking Status**: Indicates whether the student has been successfully booked for the session, which is important for scheduling and resource allocation.

### Summary
This page is a comprehensive interface for managing student attendance and feedback for a specific event. It ensures that all relevant information is displayed clearly and accessible, serving the broader system functions of event management, student tracking, and feedback collection.

## Portal Path Endpoint: Academics
- **Direct Link Target:** https://learner.saveetha.in/academicevents/event-participating/13095/#
### Structural Analysis Breakdown:
### Home Page Layout Analysis

#### 1. Actions and Specific Transactional Parameters
This page is designed to manage and display academic event participation details for a student. The specific actions and parameters controlled by this page include:
- **Event Participation**: The page allows students to view and manage their participation in academic events.
- **Feedback Submission**: Students can submit feedback on the events they attended.
- **Booking Status**: The page shows the booking status of the event.
- **Attendance Tracking**: It tracks the attendance percentage and status of the event.
- **Event Details**: It provides detailed information about the event, including the date, time, and location.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks, Table Structure, Score Layout, or Variable Value Observations
The page is structured with several key data elements and sections:
- **Header**: Displays the event title, date, and booking status.
- **Main Content Area**:
  - **Event Details**: Displays the event name, date, time, and location.
  - **Student Information**: Shows the student's name, registration number, and booking mode.
  - **Attendance Summary**: Displays the attendance percentage and status.
  - **Feedback Status**: Shows whether the feedback has been posted.
  - **Booking Info**: Provides the booking date and time.
- **Side Navigation**: Contains links to different sections like Schedule, Circulars, My Reward Points, Workflow Requests, Workflow Approvals, Bookings, Services, Mentees, and Mentorship.
- **Footer**: Contains copyright information and a loading profile indicator.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
This layout segment serves the wider system functions by:
- **Providing Detailed Information**: It ensures that students have access to all the necessary details about the events they are participating in, including the date, time, and location.
- **Facilitating Feedback Submission**: It allows students to easily submit feedback on the events they attended, which can be reviewed and acted upon by the event organizers.
- **Attendance Tracking**: It tracks the attendance percentage and status, helping the event organizers to monitor the participation levels.
- **Booking Management**: It manages the booking status of the events, ensuring that students can easily manage their bookings.
- **User Navigation**: The side navigation bar provides easy access to different sections of the system, making it user-friendly and efficient.

Overall, this layout ensures that the student portal is user-friendly, informative, and functional, providing all the necessary information and tools for students to manage their academic events effectively.

- **Feedback Status**: The student can see and update their feedback status.
- **Booking Information**: The student can view their booking details.
- **Chat**: The student can communicate with others via chat.
- **Attendance Summary**: The student can view their attendance summary.
- **Feedback Summary**: The student can view their feedback summary.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is structured into several key sections:
- **Header**: Contains the event title, date, and time.
- **Main Content Area**: Divided into sections for different types of information:
  - **Overview**: Displays the student's name, registration number, mode of participation, and session details.
  - **Chat**: A chat interface for communication.
  - **Attendance**: Displays the student's attendance status.
  - **Feedback**: Displays the student's feedback status.
  - **Summary**: Provides a summary of the student's attendance and feedback.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
This layout segment serves the wider system functions by:
- **Providing Detailed Information**: It gives students detailed information about their participation in the event, including their attendance and feedback status.
- **Facilitating Communication**: It allows students to communicate with others via chat, which is crucial for interaction and support.
- **Monitoring Progress**: It helps students monitor their progress and ensure they are meeting the event's requirements.
- **Updating Data**: It provides a platform for students to update their attendance and feedback, ensuring the system remains accurate and up-to-date.

- **Main Content Area**:
  - **Overview**: Displays the student's name, registration number, mode of booking, and session details.
  - **Summary**: Shows the student's attendance status and feedback status.
  - **Booking Info**: Provides the booking date and time.
  - **Feedback**: Indicates that the feedback has been posted.
- **Navigation Menu**: Contains links to different sections like Schedule, Circulars, My Reward Points, Workflow Requests, Workflow Approvals, Bookings, Services, Manage, Mentees, and Mentorship.
- **Footer**: Contains copyright information and a loading profile icon.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
The layout serves the following broader system functions:
- **Event Management**: Allows students to manage their participation in academic events.
- **Attendance Tracking**: Provides a clear record of student attendance.
- **Feedback Collection**: Facilitates the collection and posting of student feedback.
- **Booking Confirmation**: Ensures that students can confirm their booking details.
- **User Interface**: Offers a user-friendly interface for students to navigate and access their event details.

- **Event Details**: The event is titled "Module/LeetCode/SA" and is part of the "26-27-ODD-EXAMCENTRE - EXAMINATIONS."
- **Date and Time**: The session is scheduled from 10 a.m. to 11:29 a.m. on August 14, 2026.
- **Booking Information**: The booking was made on August 13, 2026, at 19:16.
- **Feedback Status**: The feedback has been posted, and the attendance is 100%.
- **Student Details**: The student's name is Muhammad Asjad E, with a register number of 212225240091 and a booked mode.

#### 2. Data Element Blocks and Table Structure
The page is divided into several sections:
- **Header**: Displays the event title, date, and time.
- **Sidebar**: Contains navigation links for different sections like Schedule, Circulars, My Reward Points, Workflow Requests, Workflow Approvals, Bookings, Services, and Manage.
- **Main Content Area**:
  - **Overview**: Displays the student's name, register number, and booking mode.
  - **Session Details**: Provides the event title, event term, location, and time.
  - **Summary**: Shows the attendance percentage and feedback status.

#### 3. Structural Functionality
This layout serves the following broader system functions:
- **Event Management**: Allows administrators to manage and monitor the attendance and feedback for specific events.
- **Student Tracking**: Provides a detailed view of student attendance and feedback for easy tracking and reporting.
- **User Interface**: Offers a user-friendly interface for students to view their participation details and feedback status.
- **System Integration**: Facilitates the integration of different systems for event management, student tracking, and feedback collection.
